# API field assertions — Postman-style checks on every journey request

Generated by `prototype/scripts/build-api-assertions.ts` from the responses real API journeys captured. Each case is one assertion the journey runner evaluates **live on every run** (`prototype/src/checks/apiAssertions.ts`) — the report shows it under the step as passed / failed / not checked. Required = present in every captured response; a format is claimed only when the field name names it and every observed value conforms. Do not edit by hand — re-run the script.

_Built 2026-09-27T18:06:27.739Z from 140 captured responses across 8 run(s)._

## 1. Sign in as the buyer — `GET /v1/dashboard_configuration`

Step `buyer_login` (buyer) · Postman: auth › generate otp, verify otp · seen in: RFQ only, Technical / RFP only, Technical + RFQ, Technical + Auction · 8 sample(s).

#### API-FBLOGIN-001 — The request succeeds
- **Why it matters.** Every later check reads a success body; a refusal here is the headline, not a field detail.
- **Setup.** Runs inside the API journey (RFQ only, Technical / RFP only, Technical + RFQ, Technical + Auction) on the reply to "Sign in as the buyer".
- **Steps.** Send `GET /v1/dashboard_configuration` as the journey does.
- **Expected.** HTTP 2xx.
- **Oracle.** `status_2xx` — evaluated automatically on every journey run; inferred from 8 real response(s).
- **Trace.** P0 · automate · `apiAssertions.ts` · `buyer_login`

#### API-FBLOGIN-002 — Answers within 5 s
- **Why it matters.** A regression that makes a core call several times slower is a defect even when the body is right.

- **Steps.** Send `GET /v1/dashboard_configuration` as the journey does.
- **Expected.** ≤ 5000 ms (slowest observed: 1599 ms).
- **Oracle.** `max_ms 5000` — evaluated automatically on every journey run; inferred from 8 real response(s).
- **Trace.** P2 · automate · `apiAssertions.ts` · `buyer_login`

## 3. Add the technical round (RFP) — `POST /trade/template_input`

Step `add_stage_rfp` (buyer) · Postman: event create › Template Technical · seen in: Technical / RFP only, Technical + RFQ, Technical + Auction · 5 sample(s).

#### API-FRFPSTG-001 — The request succeeds
- **Why it matters.** Every later check reads a success body; a refusal here is the headline, not a field detail.
- **Setup.** Runs inside the API journey (Technical / RFP only, Technical + RFQ, Technical + Auction) on the reply to "Add the technical round (RFP)".
- **Steps.** Send `POST /trade/template_input` as the journey does.
- **Expected.** HTTP 2xx.
- **Oracle.** `status_2xx` — evaluated automatically on every journey run; inferred from 5 real response(s).
- **Trace.** P0 · automate · `apiAssertions.ts` · `add_stage_rfp`

#### API-FRFPSTG-002 — Answers within 5 s
- **Why it matters.** A regression that makes a core call several times slower is a defect even when the body is right.
- **Setup.** Runs inside the API journey (Technical / RFP only, Technical + RFQ, Technical + Auction) on the reply to "Add the technical round (RFP)".
- **Steps.** Send `POST /trade/template_input` as the journey does.

## 20. Sign in as the evaluator — `POST /v1/verify_otp`

Step `evaluator_login` (evaluator) · Postman: auth › evaluator generate otp, evaluator verify otp · seen in: Technical / RFP only, Technical + RFQ, Technical + Auction · 5 sample(s).
